Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The membranes operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 499. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 312 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the membranes space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 499 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 312. QED.
